Art. 908: The following infractions are subject to the provisions of the present chapter:

1) infractions qualified as anticompetitive practices;

2) infractions to the rules of market transparency and restrictive practices of competition;

3) infractions to the provisions annexed to the organization of competition.

Sec~on 1:Despratiques a~ticollcurrentlelles

Art. 909 : Constitutes an anticompetitive practice, the act of contravening the rules relating to agreements and abuse of dominant position as defined by articles 910 and 911 below.

###### Art. 905

(regarding penalties incurred by any person, author or accomplice of one of the offenses listed in articles 898 to 902 above who, before any prosecution, permits the identification of other culprits or after their engagement of pursuits, permits or facilitates, the action 24 Novembre,lOIS .'JOURNAL OFFICIEL DE LAREPUBLIQUR TOGOLAISE . 115

###### Art. 910 : All agreements between enterprises, all decisions or associations of enterprises and all concerted practices having as their object or which may have the effect of preventing, restricting or distorting competition in a market, are prohibited, particularly when they tend to:

1) limit access to the market;

2) obstruct, directly or indirectly, the free setting of purchase or sale prices or other transaction conditions;

3) limit or control production, markets, investments or technical progress;

4) divide markets, customers or sources of supply;

5) apply unequal conditions to equivalent services in relation to commercial partners, thereby inflicting a disadvantage on them in competition;

6) make the conclusion of contracts subject to acceptance by partners of supplementary services which, by their nature or according to commercial usage, have no connection with the object of such contracts.

###### Art. 911 : Prohibited under the same conditions as those referred to in the preceding article is the abusive exploitation by an enterprise or group of enterprises of a dominant position or of the state of economic dependence in which a customer enterprise or a supplier is found in relation to it in the national market or a substantial part thereof, when it tends particularly to:

1) limit access to the market;

2) impose directly or indirectly unfair purchase or sale prices or other transaction conditions;

3) limit or control production, markets, investments or technical progress;

4) apply unequal conditions to equivalent services in relation to commercial partners, thereby inflicting a disadvantage on them in competition;

5) make the conclusion of contracts subject to acceptance by partners of supplementary services which, by their nature or according to commercial usage, have no connection with the object of such contracts.

These abuses may particularly consist of refusals to sell, tied sales, discriminatory practices or practices of imposed prices as well as in the abrupt, total or partial termination of established commercial relations. Abrupt termination means a termination without reasonable notice taking into account particularly the nature and duration and possible economic dependence of the enterprise victim of such termination.

24 Novembre 2015 Section 2: Of offenses against the rules of market transparency and restrictive competition practices

###### Art. 912 : Not subject to the provisions of articles 910 and 911 above are the practices:

1) which result from the application of a legislative text, subject to prior consultation and a positive opinion from the national commission on competition and consumer affairs;

2) of which enterprises can demonstrate that they have the effect of contributing to economic progress, including through the creation or maintenance of employment, and that they reserve for users an equitable share of the resulting profit, without giving the enterprises concerned the possibility of eliminating competition for a substantial part of the products in question; practices which consist of organizing, for agricultural products or of agricultural origin, under the same brand or label, the volume and quality of production as well as the commercial policy, including by agreeing on a common sale price must not impose restrictions on competition except to the extent that they are indispensable to achieve this objective of progress.

###### Art. 913 : Null and void is any commitment, agreement or contractual clause relating to a practice prohibited by articles 910 and 911 of this code.

###### Art. 914 : Liable to imprisonment of two (02) months to two (02) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, is any natural person who fraudulently takes a personal and decisive part in the design, organization or implementation of practices referred to in articles 910 and 911.

The court may jointly and severally condemn legal persons to payment of fines imposed on their managers by reason of their participation in practices prohibited by articles 910 and 911 of this code, in accordance with article 53 paragraph 6 of this code.

###### Art. 915 : Fines imposed on legal persons are calculated on the basis of a proportion of the value of sales made by each enterprise or organization in question, of products or services in connection with the offense or, if applicable, the offenses in question during the last year of participation in the offense. This proportion may represent up to fifteen percent (15%) of the value of sales made for the least serious practices, such as simple exchanges of information, from fifteen percent (15%) to thirty percent (30%) of the value of sales made for the most serious practices, such as a price cartel. This base amount is multiplied by the number of years of participation in the offense, it being understood that a period greater than or equal to six (06) months counts as one year. Fines are proportionate to the seriousness of the facts charged, the extent of the damage caused to the economy, the contributory capacity of the organization or the sanctioned enterprise and of the group to which the enterprise belongs and any recurrence of practices prohibited by articles 910 and 911 of this code.

When the practices referred to in articles 910 and 911 of this code are implemented by a legal person (enterprise or association of enterprises) under private or public law, the maximum amount of the fine incurred by that legal person is 5% of the highest worldwide turnover excluding tax achieved during one of the financial years closed since the financial year preceding the one during which the practices were committed. If the accounts of the enterprise concerned have been consolidated or combined by virtue of applicable texts in its consolidated or combined form, the turnover taken into account is that appearing in the consolidated or combined accounts of the dominant enterprise or combination.

Fines are determined individually for each sanctioned legal person, and in a reasoned manner for each sanction.

When a legal person does not contest the reality of the grievances reproached to it by reason of prohibited practices

###### Art. 918 : For the purposes of this section, considered as offenses against the rules of market transparency and as restrictive practices (enterprise or association of enterprises) are:

1) practices of imposed prices and resale at a loss;

2) non-observance of invoicing rules;

3) non-publication of price schedules and sale conditions;

4) refusal to sell or subordination of sale with regard to the consumer;

5) discriminatory practices between professionals;

6) unauthorized sales and itinerant trading;

7) non-observance of rules relating to consumer information;

8) misleading or deceptive advertising;

9) non-observance of regulations relating to direct sales to consumers.

###### Art. 919: The infractions provided for in the above article,

by articles 910 and 911 of this code, at the exception of points 2 and 8, are punishable by a penalty of imprisonment from one (01) to six (06) months and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

The tribunal may pronounce a fine taking into account the absence of contestation. In this case, the maximum amount of the sanction incurred is reduced by half.

In addition, the tribunal may order at the expense of the convicted party the publication of its decision in the newspapers it designates.

A total or partial exemption from the fine may be granted to a legal person who has, with others, implemented a practice prohibited by the provisions of articles 935 and 936 of this code, if it has contributed to establishing the reality of the prohibited practice and identifying its perpetrators, by providing evidentiary information that the tribunal did not previously have, after consultation with the national competition and consumption commission.

Likewise, is subject to the same penalty the reseller who requests from or obtains from his supplier any advantages whatsoever contrary to the rules of competition.

Without prejudice to the penalties provided for in the first paragraph above, the minister responsible for trade may, in consultation with the relevant supervising minister, order the immediate cessation of the exercise of the profession in connection with which the infraction was committed or the evacuation of public domain irregularly occupied for commercial purposes.

###### Art. 920: Notwithstanding the penalties provided for in articles 914 and 915 of this code, the competent jurisdiction may

Are deemed to be in a state of recidivism natural or legal persons who, within a period of twelve (12) years, have been found guilty of an infraction of the same nature as that for which they were definitively convicted.

order at the expense of the convicted party the publication, in full or in extracts, of its decision in one or more newspapers it designates and the posting in the places it indicates.

Any professional who sells or resells products, goods or offers services without issuing an invoice is subject to a penalty of imprisonment from one (01) to six (06) months and a fine from five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

In addition, it may prescribe the insertion of the full text of its decision in the report prepared on the operations of the enterprise by the manager or the board of directors.

2) any importation or exportation carried out in violation of the regulations for the control of goods before dispatch;

3) any importation or exportation without title or without declaration to customs of goods, products and merchandise subject to a regime or their determination;

The same applies when:

4) any use of false documents for importation or exportation purposes;

1) the invoice issued contains false information on one or more of the mentions required by law;

5) any transfer of installation authorization card and the non-possession of the installation authorization card after six (06) months of activity;

2) the invoice is false or falsified;

3) the invoice does not contain one or more of the mentions provided for by law.

6) any commercial practice without a sign affixed to the front of the building in which the activities take place and any affixing of a commercial sign whose inscriptions are unrelated to the company name shown on the installation authorization card.

Also punished with the same penalty are the non-issuance of an invoice, receipt or expense note upon request of the consumer and the non-preservation of invoices for the legal period of five (05) years from the date of the transaction.

###### Art. 924: Infractions to legislative provisions in

force relating to warranty and after-sales service are

###### Art. 921: The infractions provided for in point 8 of article 918

In addition, the obligation to perform after-sales service may be ordered by the judge.

In addition, the tribunal may order the publication of a corrective announcement at the expense of the convicted party.

Art. 925: Infractions to legislative provisions in force relating to deception, falsification and consumer safety are punished with a penalty of imprisonment from one (01) to six (06) months and a fine from one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs and/or one of these two penalties.

In all cases, the competent administration may, as precautionary measures, order the withdrawal of the disputed advertising at the expense of the defendant.

These penalties are applicable even in the case where the harmful falsification is known to the buyer or consumer.

The advertiser, on whose behalf the advertising is broadcast, is principally liable for the infraction committed.

###### Section 3: Sanctions for provisions ancillary

to the organization of competition Art. 926: The penalties provided for in article 925 are doubled if a falsified, counterfeit, corrupted or toxic substance harmful to the health of humans or animals is involved in the commission of the infraction.

###### Art. 922: Is considered an infraction to the provisions

ancillary to the organization of competition, the act of contravening legislative provisions relating to the fight against fraud, warranty and after-sales service, deception and falsification, and consumer safety.

Art. 927: Notwithstanding the provisions of articles 925 and 926 above, the merchandise, objects or apparatus whose sale, use or possession constitute infractions

###### Section 1: Illegal practice of regulated professions

Art. 935: Is considered illegal and subject to the sanctions provided for in Article 937, any practice or attempted practice of a regulated profession in violation of the conditions defined by the legislation in force.

2) the withdrawal or destruction of the products on which the infraction was committed and the prohibition of service provision;

###### Art. 936: Is prohibited and subject to the sanctions provided for in

Article 937, the fact for a person to usurp a title, function, qualification, and designation whose use is subject to legal or regulatory restrictions, either by preceding or following their name with this title, or by using, for their name or on behalf of a legal person, terms likely to create in the public an

proceedings for the infractions referred to in the preceding article, order the suspension of the sale of the product or the provision of the incriminated service.

###### Art. 937: Is punishable by imprisonment from six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, anyone who practices or attempts to practice illegally a regulated profession, usurps it or attempts to usurp it.

Any person having operated electrical or water installations intended to supply electricity or drinking water without having previously obtained an operating authorization for this purpose is punished with a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

In case of recidivism, the penalties of imprisonment and fine may be increased to double the maximum.

Any person having operated electrical or drinking water installations not intended to supply electricity or drinking water without having previously obtained an operating authorization for this purpose is punished with a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Section 2: Infractions relating to regulated sectors

###### Art. 940: Any candidate for the granting of any concession or

delegation who knowingly provides information that he knew to be false for the purpose of being granted said concession or delegation of management is punished with a fine of five million (5,000,000) to five hundred million (500,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Sous-section 1: Infractions in the handicraft sector

###### Art. 941: Is punishable by a fine of one million (1,000,000) to

###### Art. 938: Without prejudice to the provisions of this code,

three million (3,000,000) CFA francs any concessionaire or delegatee of public service holding an operating authorization who knowingly obstructs, or attempts to obstruct, by any means whatsoever the exercise by the regulatory authority and the minister responsible for energy or water, of their powers of inspection of electrical or drinking water installations determined by the legislation in force.

failure to comply with the regulations relating to handicrafts exposes offenders to the following sanctions and penalties:

1) refusal to submit to identification leads to exclusion from enjoyment of the privileges granted to artisans and artisan enterprises;

2) refusal to submit to registration or immatriculation exposes the offender to prohibition from exercising the activity;

3) false declarations at the time of registration or immatriculation lead to withdrawal of the professional card and a fine ranging from one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs according to the time of use of the false document.

###### Art. 942: Any concessionaire or delegatee of public service

who, without justification, refuses to supply electricity or drinking water to any consumer who has submitted a request to this effect in the territory covered by the concession or delegation agreement to which he is a party is punished with a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Sous-section 2: Infractions in the electricity and drinking water sectors

Any concessionaire or delegatee who, without justification,

###### Art. 939 : Any person who engages in activities for the

supply of electrical energy or drinking water in a situation where the legislation in force requires the prior conclusion of a concession or delegation agreement without having validly concluded such a concession or delegation agreement is punishable by a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs.

For the purposes of the application of paragraphs 1 and 2 above, the existence of a justification for the refusal to supply or transit is assessed with regard to:

1) the legal obligation of the concessionaire or delegatee to supply electrical energy or drinking water in accordance with the conditions stipulated in the concession or delegation agreement;

refuses any other concessionaire or delegatee or any consumer the right to transit electrical energy or drinking water through its electrical or drinking water installations is punishable by a fine of two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

The penalty referred to in the preceding point is incurred by any person operating electrical or drinking water installations, for the purpose of supplying electrical energy or drinking water and pursuant to rights acquired under the previous legislation, without having obtained or filed an application for recognition of said rights within the required time limits.

2) the legal obligation of the transmission concessionaire to allow another concessionaire to transit electrical energy or drinking water through its electrical or drinking water installations; except in cases of technical impossibility or lack of capacity to transport or transit electrical energy or drinking water.

###### Art. 943 : Any concessionaire or any delegatee or any

operator who knowingly bills any consumer or any other concessionaire or delegatee or operator, any service related to the supply of electrical energy or drinking water at prices higher than those fixed in the relevant concession or delegation agreement or in the tariff regulations is punishable by a fine of two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 944 : Is considered as constituting an offense of

corruption or influence peddling as defined in articles 594 and 608 of this code the act by:

1) any member of the regulatory authority of soliciting, accepting or facilitating by virtue of his functions, directly or indirectly, offers, promises, gifts or any advantages whatsoever with a view to favoring the granting of any concession or the issuance or renewal of any operating authorization, or the insertion in any concession agreement of more favorable stipulations, for the benefit of the author of the offers, promises, gifts or any advantages whatsoever in question, either to perform or refrain from performing an act inherent to any of his mandates, or to abuse his real or supposed influence;

2) any candidate for the granting of any concession or for the issuance or renewal of any operating authorization to make or offer, or attempt to make or offer, to any member of the regulatory authority, offers, promises, gifts or any advantages whatsoever for the purposes of obtaining from the member or members in question one of the advantages mentioned in point 1 above.

The offenses provided for in this article are punishable by a sentence of five (05) to ten (10) years of criminal imprisonment and a fine equal to double the value of the promises accepted or things received or requested, provided that said fine may not be less than five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 945 : Constitutes an illegal taking of interest within the meaning of

article 614 of this code the act by any person holding public authority or any member of the regulatory authority who, subsequent to the conclusion of any concession agreement, knowingly takes, receives or retains, directly or indirectly, any interest whatsoever in an enterprise or operation for which said concession agreement was concluded.

The offense provided for in this article is punishable by a sentence of one (01) to three (03) years of imprisonment and a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 946 : Any concessionaire or delegatee who knowingly, alone or in cooperation with other

concessionaires implements or carries out or attempts to implement or carry out, any practice having the object or effect of operating an unjustified discrimination with regard to a person or category of persons, is punishable by a fine of two million (2,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

For the application of the preceding paragraph, are considered as justifying disparities of treatment between several persons or categories of persons, the imperatives arising from technical reasons or related to the capacity of the electrical or drinking water installations of the concessionaire or delegatee concerned or any other reasons that can objectively justify such disparities.

###### Sous-section 3: Offenses relating to the communication and media sector

###### Art. 947 : Constitutes the offense of omission and is punishable by a

fine of ten thousand (10,000) to one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs, any failure to comply with the requirements relating to the declaration, printing and legal deposit with respect to the written press, the recording and preservation of broadcast or televised programs. In case of recidivism, the maximum penalty may be applied.

###### Art. 948 : Any violation of the prohibition to post

private posters in places designated by the prefect or the mayor as being exclusively intended to receive posters and acts emanating from the public authority, is punishable by a fine of five thousand (5,000) to one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs and, in case of recidivism, double this penalty.

###### Art. 949 : Whoever removes, tears, covers or alters by

any process whatsoever, so as to distort or render illegible, posters posted by order of the administration in the locations reserved for it, is punishable by a fine of five thousand (5,000) to one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs.

If the offense provided for in the preceding paragraph was committed by a civil servant or an agent of the public authority, the penalty is a fine of ten thousand (10,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 950 : Any person individually or in a group found

guilty of having posted posters or, by any process whatsoever, made inscriptions, traced signs or drawings on movable or immovable property of the State, public establishments, or property assigned to the operation of a public service, as well as any person who, without being the owner, usufructuary or tenant of a building or without being authorized by one of these persons, posts posters or, by any process whatsoever, makes inscriptions, traces signs or drawings is punishable by a fine of ten thousand (10,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 951 : In case of refusal to insert or broadcast the

response despite the summary order, the director of the publication or broadcast organ in question is liable to imprisonment of one (01) to twelve (12) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 958 : The placing on sale, distribution or reproduction

of prohibited works, the publication or broadcast under a different title of a prohibited work, when they are done with knowledge of the prohibition of the work, are punishable by a sentence of imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months with suspension and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 959 :Constitutes an offense of obstruction of press freedom

and communication any act likely to prevent the printing or free circulation of the press or to prevent journalists from freely exercising their activity.

This is punishable by imprisonment of one (01) to six (06) months with suspension, a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties for anyone guilty of offenses of this nature.

In case of recidivism, the maximum may be applied cumulatively for both penalties.

###### Art. 960: When obstruction of press freedom and communication is exercised with violence against journalists, communication technicians or against press organizations, the provisions of this Code relating to voluntary violence, destruction and degradation are applicable.

###### Sous-section 4: Offenses relating to the telecommunications and electronic communications sector

Art. 961: Is punishable by imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of fifty million (50,000,000) to five hundred million (500,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties for:

1) establishing, having established, operating or having operated a telecommunications network without the authorization provided for by the legislative and regulatory provisions in force, or maintaining it in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw this authorization;

2) providing or having the public provided with telecommunications services without the authorization provided for by the legislative and regulatory provisions in force, or maintaining it in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw this authorization;

3) establishing, having established, operating or having operated a free network or service that does not use the radio spectrum in violation of the establishment conditions obligations and the declaration obligation provided for by the legislative and regulatory provisions in force, or maintaining it in violation of a decision to suspend or stop;

4) establishing, having established, operating or having operated networks, installations, equipment and terminals and radioelectric products without the authorization provided for by the legislative and regulatory provisions in force, or in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw this authorization;

5) using a radioelectric frequency without the authorization provided for by the legislative and regulatory provisions in force, or in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw this authorization;

Art. 962: Is punishable by a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs for providing a free telecommunications service that does not use the radio spectrum in violation of the establishment conditions obligations and the declaration obligation provided for by the legislative and regulatory provisions in force, or maintaining it in violation of a decision to suspend or stop.

Art. 963: Is punishable by imprisonment of two (02) months to two (02) years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, anyone who transmits, without authorization, information or correspondence from one place to another, either using telecommunications equipment or by any other means defined by the legislative provisions in force.

###### Art. 964

Is punishable by a fine of two million (2,000,000) to eight million (8,000,000) CFA francs for

###### Art. 952: Is considered as refusal of insertion and punishable by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Also punishable by the same penalty is the act of cutting out part of the response that the publication was required to reproduce.

A suspension of publication or broadcast of fifteen (15) days to three (03) months may be pronounced against the organ in question without prejudice to the fine penalty provided for in the preceding paragraph.

Art. 953: Any owner of a press organ, any director of a publication or one of their collaborators who receives or has promised a sum of money or any other advantage for the purpose of transforming advertising into information is liable to a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

Art. 954: Any operation of a radio or television station before obtaining authorization under the conditions provided for by law is punishable by a fine of one million (1,000,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

Art. 955: The dissemination or publication of information contrary to reality with the obvious aim of manipulating consciences or distorting information or facts is punishable by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to other penalties provided for by the legislative provisions in force.

In case of recidivism, the maximum penalty may be applied.

###### Art. 956: The publication director has the obligation to require all necessary information about the authors of articles published or broadcast.

###### Art. 957

: Any person convicted of having lent in any manner whatsoever their name to the owner or to the limited partner of a publication referred to in Article 9 of the press and communication code is punishable by three (03)

122 24 November 2015 months to six (06) months imprisonment and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

In addition to the penalties provided for in the preceding paragraph, the court may:

1) order the confiscation of equipment and materials used to commit the offense;

2) order the closure of the establishment for a period of two years at most;

3) pronounce the prohibition to solicit for a period of two years at most an authorization or approval in application of the legislative and regulatory provisions in force.

The amount of the fine provided for in the preceding paragraph and resulting from cases brought at the request of the regulatory authority is shared equally between the public treasury and the regulatory authority.

Art. 967: Is punishable by imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties anyone who, by breaking cables, by damaging equipment or by any other means, voluntarily causes the interruption of telecommunications services, or voluntarily obstructs the operation of telecommunications installations and services.

Any contractual provision between two or more operators contrary to the above provision is deemed unwritten.

In case of voluntary interruption or interruption committed through negligence, the operator victim of these acts may immediately take all provisional measures, in agreement with the regulatory authority, in order to stop said acts. They may claim damages fixed by agreement of the parties or by the courts after expert opinion.

Art. 968: Is punishable by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs any person who voluntarily interferes, by using a frequency, a radioelectric installation or by any other means, with another radioelectric service.

to manufacture for the domestic market, to import for release (02) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred for consumption, to possess with a view to sale, to distribute thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs free of charge or for a fee, to connect to a network open or one of these two penalties, any person who, knowingly, to the public, or to advertise terminal equipment transmits or circulates, by means of telecommunications, and telecommunications installations without the approval false or misleading distress information or calls. provided for by law.

It may claim damages assessed by or maintaining it in violation of a decision of suspension or stoppage. agreement of the parties or by the courts based on expert opinions.

###### Art. 975: In case of conviction for one of the offenses

ArS-97 1: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of two (02) months to two (02) years and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to one hundred million (100,000,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties, anyone who transmits from one place to another, by any equipment or installation of electronic communications, information or correspondence without prior license or authorization. For the offenses provided for in Articles 969 to 974 above, the court may in addition:

1.) order the confiscation of terminal equipment and installations of electronic communications not licensed or authorized, or approved materials and installations constituting the network or enabling the provision of the service, equipment which had been intended to commit the offense or which had been used in committing it;

2) order their destruction at the expense of the convicted person;

3) order the prohibition to apply for a period of two (02) years at most, for a license, an authorization or an approval in application of the legislative provisions in force.

Art. 976: Is punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs any person who voluntarily interferes, by using a frequency or any radio installation or by any other means, with another radio service.

Art. 977: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of two (02) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, any person who, knowingly, transmits or circulates by electronic communications, information or calls of distress or false or misleading calls.

Art. 978: Is punished by a fine equal to ten (10) times the annual fee, any person who uses prefixes or numbers or blocks of numbers without prior allocation.

Art. 972: Is punished by a fine of two million (2,000,000) to eight million (8,000,000) CFA francs the act of manufacturing for the domestic market, importing for release for consumption, holding with a view to sale, distributing free of charge or for a fee, connecting to a public network or advertising terminal equipment and electronic communications installations without the approval provided for by law.

Art. 973: Is punished by a fine of five million (5,000,000) to fifty million (50,000,000) CFA francs anyone who fails to inform the regulatory authority of modifications made to the information stated in the license or authorization application.

Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one (01) to three (03) months and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, anyone who refuses to provide the regulatory authority with the information required for the proper execution of its missions, or who voluntarily provides erroneous information.

124 24 November 2015

Art. 979: The refusal to implement or provide compliance with secret conventions on cryptology, as provided by the legislative provisions in force, is punished by a fine of two million (2,000,000) to eight million (8,000,000) CFA francs.

Art. 980: Is punished by identical penalties to those of the perpetrator, any natural or legal person found guilty of complicity in the commission of any one of the offenses provided for in this subsection.

Art. 981: In case of recidivism, the penalties provided for in this subsection are doubled.

Subsection 5: Offenses relating to postal services

Art. 985: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, the act:

1) of providing or having the public provided with a postal service without the authorization provided for by law or of maintaining it in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw this authorization;

2) of creating, manufacturing or issuing stamps without the authorization provided for by law or of producing them in violation of a decision to suspend or withdraw this authorization;

3) of providing or having the public provided with a postal service in breach of the general conditions and obligations or declarations provided for by law, of maintaining it in violation of a decision to suspend or stop.

Art. 983: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, any person who:

1) fraudulently employs, affixes, deliberately removes, mutilates or counterfeits a postage stamp or part of a postage stamp;

2) sells a counterfeit postage stamp or a mutilated postage stamp.

Art. 986: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, the act:

1) of abandoning, withholding or deliberately diverting a means of transmitting mail, of impeding or delaying its operation or of obstructing or delaying the forwarding of a postal item;

2) of stopping a mail transport with the intention of stealing it or searching it;

3) of refusing or delaying the forwarding of mail or the movement of means of transmitting mail, or the modes of transport to which access depends on him.

Art. 986: Any person who fraudulently takes a personal and decisive part in the design, organization or implementation of agreements or abuse of dominant position is punished by the penalties provided for in Chapter II of Title IX.

Art. 987: In case of recidivism, the penalties provided for in Articles 982, 983, 985 and 986 above are doubled.

Art. 988: The net proceeds of fines provided for in this subsection and resulting from cases prosecuted at the request of the regulatory authority are divided equally between the public treasury and the regulatory authority.

Subsection 6: Offenses in the field of civil aviation

Art. 989: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of three (03) to six (03) months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, anyone who:

1) puts or allows in service an aircraft, drone or any other remotely controlled device which has not obtained a certificate of registration, certificate of airworthiness or exceptional permit;

2) puts or allows in service an aircraft, drone or any remotely controlled device without the identification marks provided for by law;

3) operates or allows to operate an aircraft, drone or any remotely controlled device without a certificate of airworthiness, or allows it

###### Art. 984

In case of conviction for one of the infractions provided for in articles 982 and 983, the court orders the confiscation and destruction of the aircraft, drone or any remotely controlled device, at the expense of the convicted person, as well as objects kept under conditions other than those determined by fraud and objects that obviously appear to be intended to commit the infraction, the certificate of airworthiness and the associated documents or the exceptional laissez-passer.

###### Art. 990: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of three (03) to six (06) months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, whoever:

1) operates or participates in operating an aircraft without the valid titles required by the regulations in force;

2) destroys or removes a logbook or any on-board document required by air regulations or entered in that logbook or one of these documents with inaccurate information;

3) operates an aircraft or participates in its operation under the conditions provided for in article 989 above;

4) contravenes the provisions relating to the use of duly established aerodromes;

5) operates an aircraft or participates in its operation under the influence of alcohol, narcotics or drugs.

###### Art. 991

The penalties provided for in articles 989 and 990 above are doubled if the infractions provided for in points 1, 3 and 4 of article 989 and in point 1 of article 990 were committed after refusal to issue or withdrawal of the certificate of registration, the certificate of airworthiness or the exceptional laissez-passer, or the titles required of crew members by the regulations in force.

###### Art. 992: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one (01) to two (02) months and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties:

1) whoever is on board an aircraft in flight without being able to justify their presence by a valid ticket or by the consent of the operator or the captain;

2) whoever does not comply with or refuses to comply with the captain's instructions for the safety of the aircraft or of the persons transported.

###### Article 993: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six (06) months to one (01) year and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to ten million (10,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, whoever:

1) transports by aircraft, without special authorization, explosives, weapons and ammunition of war, carrier pigeons;

24 Novembre 2015 JOURNAL OFFICIEL DE LA REPUBLIQUE TOGOLAISE 125 or

5) operates or allows to operate an aircraft, drone or any remotely controlled device under conditions of use not complying with the safety rules dictated by the legislative and regulatory provisions in force.

2) transported or uses photographic equipment whose transport and use are prohibited;

3) makes use, on board aircraft, of objects or devices whose transport is prohibited;

4) makes use of photographic or cinematographic equipment over prohibited zones without special authorization.

###### Art. 994: Whoever does not comply with the requirements relating to restrictions on overflying certain zones, is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of three (03) to six (06) months and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 995: Whoever affixes or causes to be affixed on an aircraft registration marks not conforming to those of the certificate of registration or who removes or causes to be removed, renders or causes to be rendered illegible the correctly affixed marks, is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six (06) months to two (02) years and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 996: Whoever, having been convicted for one of the infractions provided for in the preceding articles, commits an infraction falling under the present subsection or the same infraction within a period of five (05) years after expiration of the imprisonment penalty or payment of the fine or the prescription of these two penalties, is sentenced to the maximum imprisonment and fine penalties and these penalties may be doubled.

###### Art. 997: Are punished by a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs and may be punished in addition, depending on the circumstances, by a penalty of imprisonment of one (01) to two (02) months:

1) the captain who fails to keep or have kept one of the on-board documents prescribed by the regulation relating to the operation of aircraft in public air transport;

2) the crew members specifically responsible for keeping these documents;

3) the owner or lessee registered in the registration register who fails to preserve one of the on-board documents for three (03) years from the last entry;

4) those who contravene the regulations relating to the technical conditions of use of aircraft;

5) those who contravene the provisions relating to overflying cities or built-up areas.

126 24 Novembre 2015

In case of recidivism, the imprisonment penalty is pronounced.

There is recidivism when a judgment has been rendered against the offender, in the preceding year, for one of these contraventions.

###### Art. 998: Are punished by a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs, those who contravene the provisions relating to air shows.

They may be punished, in addition, depending on the circumstances, by a penalty of imprisonment of three (03) to six (06) months.

Art. 1002: All provisions of laws relating to the repression of infractions of customs regulations are applicable to goods imported or exported by aircraft under any customs regime.

All unloading and jettisoning of goods not authorized, other than those indispensable for the safety of the aircraft, are sanctioned by the penalties dictated by customs laws on smuggling.

In case of infraction and as guarantee or security for payment of the fine incurred, the aircraft may only be subject to conservatory seizure, the release of which is given if a bond is provided or a deposit is paid until

###### Art. 999

The prohibition from conducting or participating in the conduct of an aircraft may be pronounced by judgment or order, for a period of three (3) months to two (2) years, against a crew member convicted under articles 990, 993, and 994 above.

If the crew member is convicted a second time for one of these same offenses, within the period provided for in article 996, the prohibition from conducting or participating in the conduct of an aircraft is pronounced and its duration is increased to the maximum and may be raised to double.

The licenses and certificates held by the offenders remain deposited, for the entire duration of the prohibition, at the registry of the court that pronounces the prohibition.

The convicted persons shall deposit these licenses and certificates, either at this registry or at that of their domicile, within five (05) days following the date on which the conviction becomes final, failing which they shall be punished by a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs, without prejudice to the penalties provided for in article 990 above in the event that they conduct or participate in the conduct of an aircraft during the period of prohibition and which cannot be confused.

said fine.

###### Art. 1000

Any unauthorized throwing of objects from an aircraft in flight is punishable by imprisonment of three (03) to six (06) months and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million five hundred thousand (1,500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, even if this throwing caused no damage, and without prejudice to more severe penalties that could be incurred in case of other offenses.

###### Art. 1001: Any aircraft commander who,

knowing that it has just caused or occasioned an accident on the surface, does not notify without delay the authorities of the nearest airport with which he can communicate and thus attempts to escape the criminal and civil liability he may have incurred, is punished by the penalties provided for sanctioning the offense of hit and run.

24 November 2015 127 punished

###### Art. 1003: The prosecutor of the Republic, the investigating judge, the officers and agents of the judicial police designated by the code of criminal procedure, the officials of the technical bodies of civil aviation, the military or sailors and the agents of the civil or military authority commissioned for this purpose, the gendarmes and the customs agents have the right to seize explosives, weapons and war munitions, carrier pigeons, prohibited photographic equipment, photographs and postal correspondence as well as radiotelegraphic and radiotelephone equipment found on board without the special authorization provided for by the regulations. They may seize carrier pigeons, photographic equipment and photographs found on board aircraft authorized to transport these objects in the case where these aircraft have passed over prohibited zones.

The confiscation of objects and equipment lawfully seized is pronounced by the court.

###### Art. 1004: The aircraft whose certificate of airworthiness and certificate of registration cannot be produced or whose registration marks do not correspond with those of the registration certificate may be detained at the expense of the owner or, in case of lease of the aircraft registered in the registration register, at the expense of the registered lessee, by the authorities responsible for ensuring the enforcement of the civil aviation code until the identity of the owner has been established.

###### Art. 1005: The penalties provided for in articles 999 to 1004 are applicable without prejudice to the application of the provisions of the civil code and the code of civil procedure.

###### Art. 1006: Is punishable by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) anyone who, using a device, a substance or a weapon commits against a person in an airport serving international civil aviation, an act of violence which causes or is likely to cause serious injury.

###### Art. 1007: In case of recidivism, the offenses are punishable by a criminal imprisonment sentence and a fine of ten million (10,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs, anyone who:

1) destroys or damages buildings or installations intended to ensure the control of aircraft traffic, aeronautical telecommunications, aids to air navigation or meteorological assistance;

2) disrupts, by any means whatsoever, the functioning of these installations;

3) destroys or damages an aircraft within the confines of an aerodrome;

4) obstructs, in any manner whatsoever, the navigation or traffic of aircraft;

5) interrupts, using a material device, a substance or a weapon, the functioning or service of an aerodrome if this act affects or is likely to affect safety within the confines of this aerodrome;

6) communicates information he knows to be false and, by this fact, compromises the safety of an aircraft in flight.

In case of recidivism, the offenses are punishable by imprisonment of one (01) month and a fine of six hundred thousand (600,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1011: Upon requisition of the public prosecutor acting at the request of the interested minister, the court hearing the prosecution enjoins persons who violate the regulatory provisions concerning aeronautical easements, under penalty of a periodic penalty payment of five thousand (5,000) to twenty-five thousand (25,000) CFA francs per day of delay, a period to modify or remove the works subject to easements or to provide for their marking.

In the event the period is not observed, the periodic penalty payment pronounced runs from the expiration of said period until the day when the situation is effectively regularized.

The court may authorize the reversal of part of the periodic penalty payments when the situation is regularized and the debtor establishes that he was prevented from observing, by a circumstance independent of his will, the period that had been granted to him. In addition, if upon expiration of the period set by the judgment, the situation has not been regularized, the administration may have the work carried out ex officio at the expense and risk of the responsible persons.

###### Art. 1008: If the acts referred to in articles 1006 and 1007 above have resulted in the death of one or more persons, the maximum criminal imprisonment sentence may be imposed.

###### Art. 1013: Periodic penalty payments and fines are recovered by the public treasury accountants, upon requisition of

###### Art. 1009

Anyone who stays or penetrates into restricted areas of aerodromes assigned to a public service or open to public traffic, in violation of the regulations and general instructions, is punished with imprisonment of one (01) to fifteen (15) days or a fine payable immediately, established on the basis of fifty thousand (50,000) CFA francs per tonne.

The tonnage taken into account is the maximum weight at takeoff corresponding to the certificate of airworthiness of the aircraft. Any fraction of a tonne is counted as a whole tonne.

###### Art. 1010: Any person found in the reserved zone of an airport without authorization for access issued by the competent authority in aviation matters, is punished with a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs.

In case of repeat offense, the fine increases to one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties and may, in addition, be deprived of any right to indemnity in case of accident.

###### Art. 1014: Any air transport company that exercises its activity in Togo without authorization or in violation of the obligations concerning conditions of service, routes, fares, loading and unloading charges, and mounting charges prescribed by the authorizations that may have been issued, is subject to a fine of four hundred and fifty thousand (450,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties and may, in addition, be deprived of any right to indemnity in case of accident.

In case of repeat offense, the fine increases to one million (1,000,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1011: Violations of regulatory provisions concerning aeronautical easements of clearance and obstacles established in the interest of air traffic, and any violation of the provisions of the present code, are punished with a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1015: Is punished with imprisonment of one (01) to four (04) months and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million two hundred thousand (1,200,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties, any person who exercises brokerage in violation of the license granted under the provisions of the present code.

128 OFFICIAL JOURNAL OF THE TOGOLESE REPUBLIC 24 November 2015

Is punished with the same penalty the person responsible for any company that entrusts one of these positions to a person not meeting the conditions required by the civil aviation code.

The penalties provided for in this article are enacted without prejudice to the application of those of the criminal or tax laws in force. They are moreover applied without prejudice to administrative or disciplinary sanctions and damages, if applicable.

Sub-section: Offenses relating to the merchant marine

###### Paragraph 1

Crimes and offenses affecting discipline and internal police of the ship

###### Art. 1016

Is punished with imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months and a fine of thirty-six thousand (36,000) to three hundred and sixty thousand (360,000) francs CFA or one of these two penalties, any officer, master or crew member who is guilty of irregular absence from the ship when assigned to a guard or security post or when the absence has had the consequence of causing him to miss the ship's departure.

###### Art. 1017: Any captain who, except in cases of force majeure, breaks his engagement and abandons his ship before being replaced, is punished, if the ship was in safety in a port, with imprisonment of ten (10) days to two (02) years, and if the ship was in an open roadstead or at sea, with imprisonment of one (01) to two (02) year(s).

Art. 1018: Is punished with a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs any captain who is not personally present on his ship at the entry and exit of ports, harbors or rivers.

###### Art. 1019: Any captain, officer or master who abuses his authority or who orders or tolerates an abuse of authority vis-à-vis a person on board, is punished with imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs, or one of these two penalties.

Is punished with the same penalty, any captain, officer or master guilty of characterized outrage by word, gesture, or threat towards crew members.

Any captain, officer or master who, outside legitimate reasons provided by law, has used or caused to be used violence, in the exercise or on the occasion of the exercise of his duties, is punished in accordance with the provisions of the present code.

In the cases provided for in the two preceding paragraphs, the penalty may be doubled if it concerns an apprentice or a cabin boy.

###### Art. 1020: Is punished for each of the offenses referred to below with a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to two hundred thousand (200,000) CFA francs any captain who refuses or neglects without legitimate reason:

1) to make the findings required in case of crime or offense committed on board;

2) to draw up either the civil status certificates, the disappearance reports and the wills in the cases provided for by the code of persons and the family;

3) to keep regularly the logbook, the book of accounts and other regulatory documents.

###### Art. 1021: Is punished with the penalty provided by the present code for forgery, any captain, officer, master or crew member who fraudulently inscribes on the ship's documents altered facts or facts contrary to the truth.

Art. 1022: Is punished with imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months any captain who favors, by his consent, the usurpation of the exercise of command on his ship.

The same imprisonment penalty to which may be joined a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to three hundred thousand (300,000) CFA francs is pronounced against any person who has unduly taken command of a ship and against the shipowner who would be his accomplice.

Art. 1023: Any person on board, other than the captain, who commits or attempts to commit, with criminal intent and without the shipowner's knowledge, an act of fraud or contraband of a nature to result in a criminal conviction for the shipowner, is punished with imprisonment of ten (10) days to six (06) months.

If the offender is the captain, the penalty may be doubled.

Art. 1024: Is punished with imprisonment of five (05) to ten (10) years any captain who, with fraudulent intent, diverts for his profit the ship whose conduct is entrusted to him, or who, voluntarily and with criminal intent, steers a false course or destroys without necessity all or part of the cargo, provisions or ship's effects.

###### Art. 1025: Is punished with the penalty provided for in the preceding article, any captain who, with fraudulent intent, is guilty of one of the acts referred to by the commercial code, or who sells, except in the case of unseaworthiness legally established, the ship of which he has command or who carries out unloadings in contravention of legislative and regulatory provisions in force.

agents, and subject to decree authorizations regarding deposit of public funds and special laws and regulations applicable to certain natural or legal persons, it is prohibited for any natural or legal person other than a bank to solicit or accept deposits of public funds regardless of the term.

Ad, 1q~,: Is punishable by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) CFA francs to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties:

Ne sont pas considérés comme reçus du public:

1) any person who counterfeits or falsifies a cheque;

1) the funds constituting the capital of the legal person;

2) any person who knowingly uses or attempts to use a counterfeit or falsified cheque;

2) the funds received from directors of the legal person as well as shareholders, partners or members holding ten percent (10%) or more of the share capital;

3) any person who, knowingly, accepts to receive a counterfeit or falsified cheque;

3) the funds received from banks or financial institutions in connection with credit transactions;

4) the funds received from the personnel of the legal person, provided that their amount remains below ten percent (10%) of the actual equity of the legal person.

###### Art. 1081:'<< In all cases provided for in the two preceding articles, the court shall order the convicted person, for a period of one (01) to five (05) years, not to issue cheques other than those that exclusively permit withdrawal or deposit of funds by the drawer from the drawee or those that are certified.

Funds from an issue of bonds or savings certificates are always considered as deposits of funds.

This prohibition may be ordered as an interim measure by order. It shall be accompanied by an injunction requiring the convicted person to return to the bankers who had issued the formulas in their possession and in that of their agents.

3) the drawer who has not declared, under the conditions provided for, payment incidents as well as the infractions provided for by Articles 1085, 1086, 1087 and 1088 herein;

4) the drawee who contravenes the provisions of Article 1087 paragraph 2 herein and Articles 72, 74, 81 of the Uniform Law on Payment Instruments herein;

The court may order, at the expense of the convicted person, publication by extracts of the decision ordering the prohibition in the newspapers designated and according to the modalities it determines.

5) the drawee who contravenes one of the obligations listed below:

As a consequence of the aforementioned prohibition, any banker informed thereof by the Central Bank in accordance with the legislation in force, must refrain from issuing to the convicted person and their agents cheque forms other than those mentioned in the preceding paragraph.

a) the obligation to verify the identity and address of the person requesting the opening of an account and of the holder of a joint account;

b) the obligation to inform clients to whom a chequebook is issued of the sanctions incurred in case of prohibition to pay;

When the conviction is pronounced following a payment incident noted on a joint account with or without joint and several liability, the prohibition provided for in the first paragraph is automatically applicable to the other holders with respect to that account.

c) the obligation to send to its clients an account statement at least once per quarter;

d) the obligation to inquire about the situation of the applicant by consulting the file of payment incidents prior to issuing cheque forms.

Every enterprise, whatever its legal form, submitting a bid for a public procurement, must sincerely declare and communicate to the competent authority, in its response to the call for tenders, all elements allowing the identification of natural persons, residing or not on the territory of the Togolese Republic, who are the real and ultimate economic beneficiaries of the bidding enterprise in whatever legal, economic or financial form.

the failure to comply with the obligation to declare and communicate within the period of forty-five (45) days provided in paragraph 1 of this article is punished by a penalty of one (01) to six (06) months of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to two million (2,000,000) CFA francs.

Any declaration or communication made in application of this article which proves to be false, misleading or inaccurate is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

The failure to meet the obligation of declaration and communication as provided in the preceding paragraph results in the inadmissibility of the submission and is punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

The failure to comply with the obligation to declare and communicate after formal notice to regularize within thirty (30) days which may be sent by the competent authority at the end of the forty-five (45) day period provided in paragraph 1 of this article, is assimilated to a refusal to declare and communicate and punished by a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs and a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment.

Successful bidder enterprises declare to the competent authority, within sixty (60) days of its occurrence, any modification, with supporting documents, relating to the identification of the real and ultimate economic beneficiaries of these enterprises during the execution of the contract(s) and during the two (02) years following the end of their execution.

The failure to declare any modification relating to the identification of the persons referred to in paragraph 1 of this article is punished by the penalties provided in the preceding paragraph.

The failure to meet the obligation of declaration and communication as provided in the preceding paragraph is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

Any declaration and communication made in application of this article which proves to be false, misleading or inaccurate is punished by a penalty of six (06) months to two (02) years of imprisonment and a fine of five hundred thousand (500,000) to twenty million (20,000,000) CFA francs.

The sanctions referred to in this article do not exclude those that the administrative authority may take.

###### Art. 1102 : For the application of articles 1100 and 1101 of this

code, the de facto or de jure directors of the enterprises concerned may be prosecuted, as well as any natural or legal person directly or indirectly holding these enterprises, whether or not they reside on the territory of the Togolese Republic.

The real and ultimate economic beneficiaries of the enterprises concerned are punished by five times the fines and double the imprisonment penalties incurred by the persons referred to in the preceding paragraph.

#### TITLE X :OFFENCES RELATING TO

OHADA LAW

Art. 1101 :Every enterprise engaged in extractive or gas or oil exploitation activity, whatever its legal form:

Chapter 1: Offences relating to commercial company law and economic interest groupings (GIE)

1) is required to sincerely declare and communicate to the competent authority, within forty-five (45) days of the entry into force of this code, all elements allowing the identification of natural persons, residing or not on the territory of the Togolese Republic, who are the real and ultimate beneficiaries of this enterprise in whatever legal, economic or financial form;

Section 1 :Offences relating to the formation of companies

Art. 1103:Constitutes an offence the act, by the founders, the chairman and chief executive officer, the chief executive officer, the general manager or the deputy general manager of a public limited company of issuing shares before registration or at any time when registration is obtained by fraud or when the company is irregularly constituted.
