###### Art. 1128 : Are punished by a penalty of imprisonment of

three (03) months to one (01) year and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs, the presidents, administrators or general directors of companies that issue securities offered to the public who, knowingly:

1) without a notice being inserted in a newspaper authorized to receive legal announcements, prior to any publicity measure;

2) without the prospectuses and circulars reproducing the statements of the notice provided in point 1 above, and containing mention of the insertion of this notice in the newspaper authorized to receive legal announcements with reference to the number in which it was published;

3) without the posters and announcements in newspapers reproducing the same statements, or at least an extract of these statements with reference to said notice, and indications of the number of the newspaper authorized to receive legal announcements in which it was published;

4) without the posters, prospectuses and circulars mentioning the signature of the person or representative of the company from which the offer emanates and specifying whether the securities offered are listed or not and, if so, on which stock exchange.

The persons who served as intermediaries on the occasion of the transfer of securities without the prescriptions of this article having been respected are punished with the same penalty.

###### Art. 1127: Is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs, any liquidator

who, in bad faith:

1) uses the assets or credit of the company in liquidation in a way they know is contrary to its interest, for personal purposes or to favor a company in which they were interested, directly or indirectly;

2) transfers all or part of the assets of the company in liquidation to a person who had in the company the capacity of partner, general partner, manager, member of the board of directors, general administrator or auditor, without having obtained the unanimous consent of the partners or, failing that, authorization from the competent jurisdiction.

Are punished by a penalty of imprisonment of three (03) months to one (01) year and a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs, the presidents, administrators or general directors of companies that issue securities offered to the public who, knowingly:

1) do not present, within six (06) months of appointment, a report on the active and passive situation of the company in liquidation and on the continuation of liquidation operations, nor request the necessary authorizations to complete them;

2) do not prepare within three (03) months of the close of each fiscal year, the summary financial statements in view of the inventory and a written report in which they account for the operations of the liquidation during the elapsed fiscal year;

3) do not allow partners to exercise, during liquidation, their right to access company documents under the same conditions as before;

4) do not convene the partners at least once a year to report to them on the summary financial statements in case of continuation of the company's operations;

5) do not deposit in an account opened in a bank in the name of the company in liquidation, within fifteen (15) days from the distribution decision, the amounts allocated to distributions between partners and creditors;

6) do not deposit in a consignment account opened in the records of the public treasury, within one (01) year from the closing of the liquidation, the amounts attributed to creditors or partners and not claimed by them.

Chapitre II : Offenses relating to security rights law

Art. 1129: Any person guilty of a security registration effected by fraud or bearing inaccurate statements given in bad faith, is punished by a fine of three hundred thousand (300,000) to three million (3,000,000) CFA francs.

The seized jurisdiction may further order the rectification of the inaccurate statement in the terms it determines.

Art. 1130: Any person who, by fraudulent maneuvers, totally or partially deprives a lessor of their privilege on the movable property furnishing the leased premises, is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (600,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

24 Novembre 2015 JauRNALOFFICŒL 00 LAREPVBLlQUE TOGOLAISE' 145

Chapitre III : Offenses relating to simplified recovery procedures and enforcement methods

###### Art. 1131

Any debtor under seizure or any third-party holder in whose hands the seizure of corporeal property has been effected who diverts them, is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA or one of these two penalties.

The attempt to divert seized objects is punished by the same penalties.

###### Art. 1132

Any auctioneer or any other judicial auxiliary responsible for the sale of seized property, personally liable for the price of adjudications, who receives a sum above the bid, is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) year(s) and a fine of one hundred thousand (100,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

##### Chapitre IV : Offenses relating to collective procedures for clearing liabilities

###### Section 1: Bankruptcy

###### Paragraphe 1 : Simple bankruptcy

Article 1134: Any person guilty of simple bankruptcy is punished by a penalty of imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) year(s) and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Paragraphe 2 : Fraudulent bankruptcy

Art. 1135 : Constitutes fraudulent bankruptcy, the act by a natural person in a state of cessation of payments of:

1) concealing their accounting;

2) diverting or dissipating all or part of their assets;

3) acknowledging themselves, either in their records, or by public acts or commitments under private signature, or in their balance sheet, as debtor of sums they did not owe;

4) exercising the commercial profession contrary to a prohibition provided by uniform acts or by law;

5) paying a creditor to the prejudice of the estate after cessation of payments;

6) stipulating with a creditor particular advantages by reason of their vote in the deliberations of the estate or entering into a particular agreement with a creditor from which would result for the latter an advantage at the expense of the debtor's assets from the day of the opening decision.

###### Art. 1133: Constitutes simple bankruptcy the act for a natural person in a state of cessation of payments of:

1) presenting or having presented in bad faith, a statement of results, a balance sheet, a statement of claims and debts or a statement of assets and liabilities, privileges and securities, that is inaccurate or incomplete;

2) accomplishing without the authorization of the president of the competent jurisdiction, or attempting to accomplish, one of the following acts prohibited by the competent jurisdiction:

a) paying, in whole or in part, claims arising more than thirty (30) days prior to the decision suspending individual proceedings, without legitimate excuse;

b) making use of a disposition foreign to the normal operation of the enterprise or granting a security having regard to the importance of the enterprise;

c) satisfying creditors who have paid claims arising prior to the decision suspending individual proceedings.

###### Art. 1134: Any person guilty of simple bankruptcy is punished by imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) years and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1135: For the purposes of this article and the preceding article, the term "managers" means all de jure or de facto managers and, in a general manner, any person having directly or through an intermediary, administered, managed or liquidated the legal person under cover or in place of its legal representatives.

###### Section 2: Offences assimilated to simple bankruptcy

###### Art. 1136: Constitutes also a fraudulent bankruptcy, the act for a natural person on the occasion of a judicial settlement procedure of:

1) contracting without receiving values in exchange, commitments deemed too important having regard to their situation when they were contracted;

2) intentionally delaying the finding of cessation of payments by making purchases with a view to resale below market price or employing ruinous means to obtain funds;

3) having paid a creditor to the prejudice of the body of creditors, after cessation of payments;

4) having an incomplete or irregular accounting or keeping no accounting in conformity with accounting rules and recognized customs of the profession having regard to the importance of the enterprise;

5) having been declared twice in a state of cessation of payments within a period of five (5) years, these procedures having been closed for insufficiency of assets.

###### Art. 1137: Any person guilty of fraudulent bankruptcy is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

146 JOURNAL OFFICIEL DE LA REPUBLIQUE TOGOLAISE 24 Novembre 2015

###### Art. 1138: Constitutes an offence assimilated to simple bankruptcy the act for natural persons managing legal persons subject to collective procedures and natural persons who are permanent representatives of legal persons managing legal persons subject to collective procedures, in this capacity and in bad faith, of:

1) consuming sums belonging to the legal person by engaging in games of chance or fictitious operations;

2) making purchases with a view to resale below market price or employing ruinous means to obtain funds with the intention of delaying the finding of cessation of payments of the legal person;

3) paying or causing to be paid a creditor to the prejudice of the body of creditors, after cessation of payments of the legal person;

4) causing the legal person to contract, on behalf of another, without it receiving values in exchange, commitments deemed too important having regard to its situation when these were contracted;

5) keeping, causing to be kept or allowing to be kept irregularly or incompletely the accounting of the legal person or keeping no accounting in conformity with accounting rules and recognized customs of the profession having regard to the importance of the enterprise;

6) failing to make at the registry of the competent jurisdiction, within the period of thirty (30) days, the declaration of the state of cessation of payments of the legal person;

7) diverting or concealing, attempting to divert or conceal a part of their property or fraudulently acknowledging themselves to be debtors of sums they did not owe in order to remove all or part of their assets from the proceedings of the legal person in a state of cessation of payments or those of the partners or creditors of the legal person.

###### Art. 1139: Any person guilty of an offence assimilated to simple bankruptcy is punished by imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) years and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

Paragraph 2: Offences assimilated to fraudulent bankruptcy

###### Art. 1142: Constitutes an offence assimilated to fraudulent bankruptcy the act for natural persons managing legal persons subject to collective procedures and natural persons who are permanent representatives of legal persons managing legal persons subject to collective procedures, of fraudulently:

1) removing the books of the legal person;

2) diverting or concealing a part of its assets;

3) acknowledging the legal person to be a debtor of sums it did not owe, either in the books, or by public acts or commitments under private signature, or in the balance sheet;

4) exercising the profession of manager contrary to a prohibition provided for by the Uniform Acts or by law;

5) stipulating with a creditor, in the name of the legal person, special advantages by reason of their vote in the deliberations of the body of creditors or making with a creditor a particular agreement from which would result for the latter an advantage to the charge of the assets of the legal person, from the day of the decision declaring the cessation of payments.

24 Novembre 2015 147

###### Art. 1143: Constitutes also an offence assimilated to fraudulent bankruptcy, the act for natural persons managing legal persons subject to collective procedures and natural persons who are permanent representatives of legal persons managing legal persons subject to collective procedures, on the occasion of a preventive settlement procedure of:

1) contracting without receiving values in exchange, commitments deemed too important having regard to the situation of the legal person when they were contracted;

2) intentionally delaying the finding of cessation of payments by making purchases with a view to resale below market price or employing ruinous means to obtain funds;

3) having kept, caused to be kept or allowed to be kept irregularly or incompletely the accounting of the legal person or having kept no accounting in conformity with accounting rules and recognized customs of the profession having regard to the importance of the enterprise;

4) having diverted or concealed, attempted to divert or conceal a part of their property or fraudulently acknowledged themselves to be debtors of sums they did not owe in order to remove all or part of their assets from the proceedings of the legal person in a state of cessation of payments or those of the partners or creditors of the legal person.

###### Art. 1144: Any person guilty of an offence assimilated to fraudulent bankruptcy is punished by imprisonment of one (01) to five (05) years and a fine of two million (2,000,000) to six million (6,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1145: Any legal or de facto representative of a legal person comprising partners indefinitely and jointly liable for the debts thereof, who, without legitimate excuse does not make at the registry of the competent jurisdiction, within the period of thirty (30) days, the declaration of their state of cessation of payments or if this declaration does not include the list of joint and several partners with the indication of their names and domiciles, is punished by imprisonment of one (01) month to two (02) years and a fine of two hundred thousand (200,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

###### Art. 1146: Any spouse, descendant, ascendant, collateral relative or relative by marriage of the debtor who, without their knowledge, diverts, converts or conceals effects belonging to the assets of said debtor in a state of cessation of payments, is punished by imprisonment of six (06) months to three (03) years and a fine of fifty thousand (50,000) to five hundred thousand (500,000) CFA francs or one of these two penalties.

###### Art. 1195: Anyone who, by granting or promising a financial

accounts within a period of three (03) months after the end of the fiscal year; or material advantage, influences or attempts to influence the exercise, whether mentioned or not, of the right or freedom of one or more voters, directly or indirectly, is punished by a sentence of six (06) months to three (03) years of imprisonment and a fine of one million (1,000,000) to five million (5,000,000) CFA francs.

3) any party leader, any candidate and any member of a list of candidates who benefited from State subsidy for electoral campaigns, who does not prepare a financial report or who failed to file a financial report presenting the campaign accounts, accompanied by supporting documents for the resources and expenses incurred, with the Court of Accounts within a period of three (03) months after the publication of the final election results.

In case of conviction, the interested parties are deprived of their right to vote for a period of three (03) years.

###### Art. 1196: Any person may report to the public prosecutor

the acts referred to in the preceding article. An additional penalty of ineligibility may be pronounced for a period of three (03) years.

###### Art. 1191: Any involuntary non-compliance with the law or

regulations relating to the organization or conduct of electoral operations is punished by a fine of two hundred and fifty thousand (250,000) to one million (1,000,000) CFA francs.

member of a list of candidates who receives, directly or indirectly, material or financial assistance from a foreign government, from a natural or legal person of foreign nationality, from a foreign political party, from a foreign non-profit organization, except for organizations composed mainly of natural persons of Togolese nationality, or from an international organization, intended for the financing of the activities of a political party or an electoral campaign;

###### Paragraphe 1 : Public financing

2) any natural or legal person of foreign nationality, except for foreign non-profit organizations composed mainly of natural persons of Togolese nationality, who offers, promises, or gives, to any political party, alliance, coalition or grouping of political parties, any candidate and any member of a list of Togolese candidates, material or financial assistance intended for the financing of the activities of a political party or an electoral campaign.

###### Art. 1200 : Is punished by a fine of seven million

(7,000,000) to thirty million (30,000,000) CFA francs and by imprisonment of six (06) months to one (01) year or by one of these two penalties, without prejudice to the civil and administrative sanctions provided by law:

#### TITRE XIV

TRANSITIONAL AND FINAL PROVISIONS Art. 1205 : This law shall be published in the Official Journal and

executed as law of the State.

###### Art. 1203 : Convictions to life imprisonment

Made in Lomé on 24 November 2015 pronounced by the competent courts, which have become final on the date of entry into force of this code, are commuted by operation of law into a sentence of fifty (50) years of criminal imprisonment.
